The unwired strings are around lines ~274–282 of `site/public/index.html`:
- `<a class="btn btn-ghost" href="#join">See it in action + a real reel</a>`
- `<span class="chip"><b>1.</b> Auto-index rallies</span>`
- `<span class="chip"><b>2.</b> Dead time removed</span>`
- `<span class="chip"><b>3.</b> Download your reel</span>`
- also check the `<h3>Dead time removed</h3>` (~line 329) and any sibling hardcoded hero/feature copy in the same block.

Task: wire these through the shim like the rest of the page — add `site.hero.demo.*` (or similar) keys to `/locales/en/common.json`, add `data-i18n`/`data-i18n-html` attributes (use `data-i18n-html` where there's inline `<b>`), then translate into kn, hi, es, da, id (AI-draft → adversarial verify → revise, reusing the established per-locale vocabulary: rally = kn ರ್ಯಾಲಿ / hi रैली / es jugada / da duel / id reli; "reel" kept). Keep brand/tokens literal.

Process discipline (the repo has fast-churning shared checkouts + concurrent sessions): work in an ISOLATED git worktree branched from `origin/master` (`git fetch origin && git worktree add -b <branch> <path> origin/master`); stage explicit paths only; re-verify `git log origin/master..HEAD` lists only your commits before opening the PR. Run `npm run copy:locales` (site), and the full site (`node --test`) + web (`vitest run`, typecheck, coverage, build) suites green. The byte-for-byte HTML↔en drift guard (`site/src/assets.test.js`) and the tag-multiset integrity guard (`site/src/i18n.shim.test.js`) will validate your wiring. Extend the assets/shim tests to cover the new keys. Translations stay native-review-pending (pre-launch gate). Reference the closed PRs #86 (own-model kn/hi) and #87 (es/da/id site) for the exact pattern.

9	
10	After B3 (#70, 2026-06-21), the localization catalogs are the **single source of truth at repo-root `/locales/{en,kn,hi,es,da,id}/common.json`** (D-CATALOG-HOME). Both surfaces consume them:
11	
12	- **SPA (`web/`):** `web/src/i18n/index.ts` imports `../../../locales/<lng>/common.json` directly. Vite resolves the workspace root via the repo `.git` marker, so dev/build/vitest all read the parent-dir JSON. `web/src/i18n/hygiene.test.ts` reads `/locales` for key-parity + placeholder-parity (+ an es 3-form plural check). Adding a key to en means adding it to ALL locales or these tests fail.
13	- **Marketing site (`site/`, dependency-free):** `site/scripts/copy-locales.mjs` mirrors `/locales` → `site/public/locales/` (`npm run copy:locales`; `predeploy` hook). The committed copy is what ships; `site/src/locales-drift.test.js` fails CI if it drifts. The shim `site/public/i18n.js` (vanilla `t()`/`data-i18n`, ICU-lite via `Intl.PluralRules`) fetches those at runtime; `server.js` does Accept-Language negotiation (cookie→?lang→Accept-Language→en) and stamps `<html lang>`.
14	
15	**Negotiation contract (both surfaces, identical):** persisted (`localStorage`/cookie key `khelsutra.lang`) → `?lang=` → navigator/Accept-Language → `en`; a missing key degrades to en, never a raw key.
16	
17	**Marketing content is en-only so far (B3):** the `site.*` namespace in the catalogs is authored in en; non-en `site.*` values are en seeds **pending translation (tracker A3)**. The SPA console strings (app/query/grid/footer/…) ARE translated for kn/hi/es/da/id. Because the SPA bundles the whole `common.json`, it also ships the (unused) `site.*` keys — negligible.
18	
19	Catalog files are a **contention hotspot** across i18n PRs — see [[shared-checkout-concurrency]].
20

9	
10	The `khelsutra` repo is worked by **multiple concurrent sessions on ONE shared working checkout** (`C:/Users/avidu/Projects/khelsutra`). During the 2026-06-21 B3 session, master advanced **5+ times** (#67→#68→#69→#71→#70) within ~1 hour, and a sibling **A9** session had **uncommitted catalog WIP sitting in the shared working tree** that carried across a `git checkout`.
11	
12	**Why:** the owner runs several parallel "slates"; the shared tree + current branch can shift under you between two commands, and sibling WIP must never be clobbered (a past incident had a squash-merge silently absorb sibling work).
13	
14	**How to apply (for any repo-writing task here):**
15	- **Don't work in the shared checkout when a sibling may be active.** Create an isolated worktree: `git worktree add <path> -b <branch> origin/master` (or check out an existing branch). Run `npm ci` per package there (web/ + site/ each have their own `node_modules`; not shared across worktrees). Clean up after merge: `git worktree remove <path> --force`.
16	- **Always branch from `origin/master`** and re-verify `git log origin/master..HEAD` shows ONLY your commits before opening a PR.
17	- **Stage explicit paths**, never `git add -A`.
18	- **Expect master to move mid-task.** If your PR shows `mergeable=false` but `git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/master HEAD` is true, GitHub's mergeability is just **stale** — collapse to one clean commit on top of latest `origin/master` (`git reset --soft origin/master` + recommit) and `git push --force-with-lease` to force a fresh recompute (this also triggers the `test` workflow, which GitHub **skips while a PR is CONFLICTING**).
19	
20	See [[catalog-home-and-i18n-layout]] for the i18n-specific contention (catalog files).
21

393	        <h2 style="margin-top:.5rem">Most academy halls run several courts at once</h2>
394	        <p style="color:var(--mut);font-size:1.05rem">Academies already record a lot of video — and then almost never watch it, because cutting it into useful clips by hand is tedious. The good rallies stay buried.</p>
395	        <p style="color:var(--mut);font-size:1.05rem">A single recording of a busy, multi-court hall is exactly where ordinary video tools choke. Pulling the <b style="color:var(--tx)">real</b> rallies out of busy, multi-court footage is the hard problem KhelSutra is built for. It already works very well on clean, single-court recordings — and the multi-court case is what we're sharpening now.</p>
396	      </div>
397	      <div class="panel">
398	        <span class="pill">Capture tip</span>
399	        <h2 style="font-size:1.4rem">Best results: a steady, full-court shot</h2>
400	        <p>Camera behind the court, slightly elevated, whole court in frame. Keep it still — a fixed tripod beats handheld every time. Shoot landscape, record the whole session, upload as-is. KhelSutra does the cutting.</p>
401	      </div>
402	    </div>
403	  </div>
404	</section>
405	
406	<section id="sharper" style="background:#EFF4EC">
407	  <div class="wrap">
408	    <div class="sec-head">
409	      <div class="kicker">What makes it different</div>
410	      <h2>It gets sharper from real academy footage</h2>
411	      <p>The footage real academies contribute — especially the busy multi-court halls — is what makes KhelSutra better over time. Early users aren't just customers; they're co-builders.</p>
412	    </div>

415	        <div class="ic" aria-hidden="true">↗</div>
416	        <h3>Improves with the library</h3>
417	        <p>When you choose to share footage and coaches confirm what it found, that growing labelled library is what sharpens future versions — rather than staying frozen.</p>
418	      </div>
419	      <div class="card">
420	        <div class="ic" aria-hidden="true">⛉</div>
421	        <h3>Your data stays yours</h3>
422	        <p>We never use your video to improve the tool without your clear, explicit opt-in. We never publish your footage. You decide what, if anything, you share.</p>
423	      </div>
424	      <div class="card">
425	        <div class="ic" aria-hidden="true">♦</div>
426	        <h3>Minors are protected</h3>
427	        <p>Footage involving minors is never used to improve the tool and is never published — full stop.</p>
428	      </div>
429	    </div>
430	    <div class="honest">
431	      <b>Read this honestly:</b>
432	      <p>Today the tool gives you rally detection, reels, and summaries. It does not retrain itself as you use it, and there's no per-person tracking. "Gets sharper over time" is the model we're building toward, powered by contributed footage — not a switch that's already on.</p>
433	    </div>

466	      <h2>Toward an AI assistant coach</h2>
467	      <p>Fed by the growing video library, KhelSutra is heading from "finds rallies" to a genuine training companion. This is the direction — not today's buttons.</p>
468	    </div>
469	    <div class="road">
470	      <div class="step"><div class="n">◷</div><h3>Shot detection</h3><p>Smashes, drops, net shots, serves and clears within each rally.</p></div>
471	      <div class="step"><div class="n">◔</div><h3>Deeper analysis</h3><p>Per-rally and per-match patterns — what's working and what isn't.</p></div>
472	      <div class="step"><div class="n">◑</div><h3>Per-match history</h3><p>Track progress over weeks and months, not just one clip.</p></div>
473	      <div class="step"><div class="n">◕</div><h3>Assistant coach</h3><p>Surfaces what to work on next — supports the real coach, never replaces.</p></div>

1	// Unit tests for the marketing-site i18n shim (public/i18n.js, tracker B3). Proves the shim mirrors
2	// the SPA's react-i18next contract: same negotiation order, ICU-lite interpolation/plurals, en
3	// fallback, and a data-i18n walker that NEVER leaves a raw key. Tested against the REAL shared
4	// catalogs (/locales) — so this also proves both surfaces consume one source of truth.
5	import { test } from "node:test";
6	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
7	import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";

9	import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";

12	const LOCALES = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "locales");
13	const load = (loc) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES, loc, "common.json"), "utf8"));
14	const catalogs = { en: load("en"), kn: load("kn"), hi: load("hi"), es: load("es"), da: load("da"), id: load("id") };
15	
16	test("negotiation order matches the SPA: persisted -> ?lang -> navigator -> en", () => {
17	  assert.equal(negotiate({ stored: "kn", query: "hi", nav: ["en-US"] }), "kn", "persisted pref wins");
18	  assert.equal(negotiate({ query: "hi", nav: ["en-US"] }), "hi", "?lang beats navigator");
19	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["kn-IN", "en-IN"] }), "kn", "navigator picks the first supported");
20	  assert.equal(negotiate({}), "en", "nothing -> en");
21	});
22	
23	test("region subtags normalize and unsupported degrades to en (never a locale we can't serve)", () => {
24	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["hi-IN"] }), "hi", "hi-IN -> hi");
25	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["ta-IN"] }), "en", "Tamil (unsupported) -> en");
26	  assert.equal(negotiate({ stored: "es" }), "es", "es is now a supported site locale (parity with the SPA's 6)");
27	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["da-DK"] }), "da", "da-DK -> da");
28	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["id-ID"] }), "id", "id-ID -> id");
29	  assert.deepEqual([...SUPPORTED], ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]);
30	  assert.equal(DEFAULT, "en");
31	});
32	
33	test("ICU-lite interpolation: {name} substitution", () => {
34	  assert.equal(interpolate("over {n}s", { n: 10 }), "over 10s");
35	  assert.equal(interpolate("Couldn't load: {error}", { error: "boom" }), "Couldn't load: boom");
36	});

40	  assert.equal(interpolate(en, { count: 1 }, "en"), "1 rally selected");

126	const flatOwn = (obj, p = "") => Object.entries(obj).reduce((a, [k, v]) => {
127	  const key = p ? `${p}.${k}` : k;
128	  if (v && typeof v === "object") Object.assign(a, flatOwn(v, key)); else a[key] = v; return a;
129	}, {});

180	    // The vast majority of the marketing copy must differ from the en seed (a handful of cognates like
181	    // "Flyer"/"FAQ"/"Roadmap"/"Net" legitimately stay English in da/id — allow a small margin).
182	    const stillEn = target.filter((k) => locSite[k] === enSite[k]);
183	    assert.ok(stillEn.length <= 8, `${loc}: ${stillEn.length} marketing keys still equal the en seed (untranslated?): ${stillEn.slice(0, 10).join(", ")}`);
184	    // Concrete prose anchors across the surfaces MUST be translated (never an en seed).
185	    for (const key of ["hero.lead", "record.intro", "capture.consent", "ownModel.heroLead", "ownModel.train.honestBody"]) {
186	      assert.notEqual(locSite[key], enSite[key], `${loc} site.${key} must be translated, not an en seed`);
187	    }
188	    // The Request-Access form (site.join.*) is intentionally English in EVERY locale (A6 deferred) — parity with kn/hi.
189	    assert.equal(locSite["join.gpuLabel"], enSite["join.gpuLabel"], `${loc} site.join.* must stay English (A6 deferred, same as kn/hi)`);
190	  }
191	});

193	test("site: es/da/id keep en's inline-HTML tag-set + load-bearing literal tokens (no markup/token drift)", () => {
194	  const enSite = flatOwn(catalogs.en.site);
195	  const knSite = flatOwn(catalogs.kn.site);
196	  const target = Object.keys(enSite).filter((k) => knSite[k] !== enSite[k]);
197	  const tagset = (s) => (String(s).match(/<[^>]+>/g) || []).slice().sort().join("|");
198	  for (const loc of ["es", "da", "id"]) {
199	    const locSite = flatOwn(catalogs[loc].site);
200	    for (const key of target) {
201	      assert.equal(tagset(locSite[key]), tagset(enSite[key]), `${loc} site.${key}: inline HTML tag-set drifted from en`);
202	    }
203	    // Brand / technical tokens survive translation verbatim across the site.
204	    assert.match(locSite["hero.lead"], /KhelSutra/, `${loc} hero.lead lost the brand`);
205	    assert.match(locSite["record.settings.body"], /1080p\+|30 fps/, `${loc} record.settings.body lost the technical tokens`);
206	    assert.match(locSite["ownModel.annotate.body"], /rally-annotator/, `${loc} own-model annotate.body lost rally-annotator`);
207	    assert.match(locSite["ownModel.setup.reasons"], /service_fault/, `${loc} own-model setup.reasons lost the ending_reason enum`);
208	    assert.match(locSite["reveal.shareText"], /khelsutra\.guru/, `${loc} reveal.shareText lost the URL`);
209	  }

244	    // Order matters: the Latin display font leads, then the Noto Indic families, BEFORE the generic

246	    const stack = html.match(/--font:([^;}]*)/)[1];

248	    assert.ok(stack.indexOf("'Noto Sans Devanagari'") < stack.indexOf("system-ui"), `${f}: Noto Devanagari must precede system-ui`);

254	  const dig = (obj, key) => key.split(".").reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? o : o[k]), obj);
255	  // Both the rich (data-i18n-html, inline <b>) and plain (data-i18n) forms are used, and EVERY key
256	  // across BOTH forms resolves in the en source catalog (a typo'd key would silently show English).
257	  for (const f of ["index.html", "capture.html"]) {
258	    const html = read(f);

260	    for (const key of keys) {
261	      assert.notEqual(dig(enCatalog, key), undefined, `${f}: data-i18n key "${key}" has no en catalog entry`);
262	    }

265	  const cap = read("capture.html");

270	  // en catalog's recording values keep the inline <b> markup the HTML relies on.

275	test("A3: EVERY data-i18n[-html] HTML default matches the en catalog byte-for-byte (no drift, incl. rich <b> keys)", () => {
276	  // The rich (data-i18n-html) keys are the most drift-prone: the inline <b> markup must match the
277	  // catalog exactly, or an en visitor's no-JS default and the shim's en render silently diverge.
278	  // Parse the REAL pages in jsdom and compare each tagged element's default to enCatalog.
279	  const dig = (o, k) => k.split(".").reduce((a, c) => (a == null ? a : a[c]), o);
280	  let checked = 0;
281	  for (const f of ["index.html", "capture.html", "own-model.html"]) {
282	    const { document } = new JSDOM(read(f)).window;
283	    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
284	      if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue; // attribute defaults aren't the visible text
285	      const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
286	      const key = htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
287	      const enVal = dig(enCatalog, key);
288	      assert.notEqual(enVal, undefined, `${f}: data-i18n key "${key}" missing from en catalog`);
289	      const got = (htmlKey ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
290	      assert.equal(got, enVal, `${f}: "${key}" HTML default drifted from the en catalog`);
291	      checked++;
292	    }
293	  }
294	  assert.ok(checked >= 60, `expected to check the full localized surface, only saw ${checked}`);
295	});

300	  const om = read("own-model.html");
301	  const keys = [...om.matchAll(/data-i18n(?:-html)?="([^"]+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]).filter((k) => k.startsWith("site.ownModel."));
302	  // The whole page body (hero, annotate/train/setup, "what this is not", CTA, footer) is now localizable.
303	  assert.ok(keys.length >= 40, `own-model should wire its body content (found only ${keys.length} site.ownModel keys)`);
304	  for (const key of keys) assert.notEqual(dig(enCatalog, key), undefined, `own-model data-i18n key "${key}" has no en catalog entry`);
305	  // Rich (inline-markup) and plain forms are both used.
306	  assert.match(om, /data-i18n-html="site\.ownModel\.heroLead"/, "the hero lead should localize");
307	  assert.match(om, /data-i18n-html="site\.ownModel\.train\.honestBody"/, "the honest-framing fence should localize");
308	  assert.match(om, /data-i18n="site\.ownModel\.not\.title"/, "the 'what this is not' heading should localize");
309	  // Code / CLI blocks deliberately stay LITERAL (commands, paths, CSV) — never wired for translation.
310	  assert.doesNotMatch(om, /<pre[^>]*data-i18n/i, "code blocks must stay literal (not wired for translation)");
311	  assert.match(om, /python -m backend\.worker train/, "the developer CLI command must stay verbatim in the page");
312	  // The honest framing survives in the en DOM default (server smoke + no-JS reads rely on it).
313	  assert.equal(enCatalog.site.ownModel.train.honestTitle, "Read this honestly.");
314	});
315	
316	// ---- A6: plain-language Request-Access GPU/storage questions ------------------------------------
317	test("A6: the GPU/storage questions are de-jargoned + reassuring, backend contract intact", () => {
318	  const html = read("index.html");
319	  // The intimidating phrasings are gone from the visible copy.
320	  assert.doesNotMatch(html, /GPU machine/i, "'GPU machine' jargon should be gone");
321	  assert.doesNotMatch(html, /process video locally/i, "'process video locally' jargon should be gone");
322	  assert.doesNotMatch(html, /\(S3\/GCS\)/, "the bare '(S3/GCS)' jargon should be gone");
323	  // Reassurance: a non-tech user is explicitly told a GPU isn't required.
324	  assert.match(html, /run it in the cloud for you/i, "the GPU question must reassure that the cloud is an option");
325	  assert.match(enCatalog.site.join.gpuHint, /no worries|don't need|cloud/i, "the GPU hint should reassure");
326	  // Questions route through the shim (translation-ready).
327	  assert.match(html, /data-i18n="site\.join\.gpuLabel"/, "GPU question not wired for i18n");
328	  assert.match(html, /data-i18n="site\.join\.storageLabel"/, "storage question not wired for i18n");
329	  // The backend contract is preserved: field names + EVERY enum value unchanged (server validates these).
330	  for (const v of ["yes", "unsure", "no", "prefer-cloud"]) assert.match(html, new RegExp(`name="has_gpu" value="${v}"`), `has_gpu value ${v} lost`);
331	  for (const v of ["gdrive", "onedrive", "dropbox", "bucket", "local", "unsure"]) assert.match(html, new RegExp(`name="storage" value="${v}"`), `storage value ${v} lost`);
332	});
333	
334	// ---- A7: camera-placement diagram on the capture page ------------------------------------------
335	test("A7: the capture page shows an accessible, localized camera-placement diagram", () => {
336	  const cap = read("capture.html");
337	  assert.match(cap, /<figure class="diagram">/, "the placement diagram figure is missing");
338	  assert.match(cap, /<svg[^>]*role="img"/, "the diagram must be an accessible <svg role=\"img\">");
339	  assert.match(cap, /<title data-i18n="site\.capture\.diagram\.title"/, "the svg needs a localized accessible <title>");
340	  assert.match(cap, /fill-opacity="0\.10"/, "the field-of-view cone (what the camera captures) is missing");
341	  assert.match(cap, /≈30°/, "the ~30° downward-tilt annotation is missing");
342	  assert.match(cap, /①[\s\S]*②[\s\S]*③/, "the diagram's step markers ①②③ are missing");
343	  // The legend maps the markers to the placement rules and is fully localizable.
344	  for (const k of ["title", "camera", "angle", "frame", "net"]) {
345	    assert.match(cap, new RegExp(`data-i18n="site\\.capture\\.diagram\\.${k}"`), `diagram lacks the ${k} label`);
346	    assert.notEqual(enCatalog.site.capture.diagram[k], undefined, `en catalog missing diagram.${k}`);
347	  }
348	  // The diagram complements the existing #1 "Place the camera" text step (doesn't replace it).
349	  assert.match(cap, /data-i18n="site\.capture\.step1\.title"/, "the placement text step should remain");
350	});
351	
352	// ---- A2: localized WhatsApp share in the post-signup reveal -------------------------------------
353	test("A2: the post-signup reveal offers a localized, safe WhatsApp share of the reel", () => {
354	  const html = read("index.html");
355	  // The reveal is injected after sign-up, so its share copy comes from the shim bridge
356	  // (window.KhelSutra.t) with an en fallback, rendered as a wa.me link.
357	  assert.match(html, /window\.KhelSutra\s*&&\s*window\.KhelSutra\.t/, "reveal should pull share copy from the shim bridge");
358	  assert.match(html, /ksT\('site\.reveal\.shareText'\)/, "reveal should use the site.reveal.shareText key");
359	  assert.match(html, /id="reel-share"/, "the reveal share button is missing");
360	  assert.match(html, /'https:\/\/wa\.me\/\?text='/, "share must be a wa.me link");
361	  assert.match(html, /rel="noopener noreferrer"/, "external share link must be tab-nabbing-safe");
362	  // The shim exposes the bridge after render.
363	  assert.match(read("i18n.js"), /window\.KhelSutra\s*=\s*\{\s*t:/, "the shim must expose window.KhelSutra.t for dynamic content");
364	  // Keys exist in the shared catalog (en authored; kn/hi translated).
365	  assert.ok(enCatalog.site.reveal.share && enCatalog.site.reveal.shareText, "en catalog missing site.reveal.*");
366	});

hero.title : Every rally, indexed. <span class="hl">The dead time, gone.</span>
hero.lead  : KhelSutra finds every rally in your session and strips out the knock-ups, walks and gaps — so a full match becomes a clean highlight reel you can watch and download. No editing, no scrubbing.
hero.ctaHow: See how it works
record.intro: Highlight quality starts with the recording. Set the camera up once like this and KhelSutra has everything it needs to find the rallies.
record.upload.body: Even lighting, then record the <b>whole session</b> and upload it as-is — KhelSutra finds the rallies and does the cutting.
ownModel.heroLead: You can't train Gemini or GPT on your court footage. KhelSutra's direction is the inverse — a small, <b style="color:#EAF3EE">owned</b> rally model where the moat is your consented dataset, on a commercially-clean, inspectable stack you control.

hero.title : ಪ್ರತಿ ರ್ಯಾಲಿಯೂ ಸೂಚಿಯಲ್ಲಿ. <span class="hl">ನಿಷ್ಕ್ರಿಯ ಸಮಯ, ಮಾಯ.</span>
hero.lead  : KhelSutra ನಿಮ್ಮ ಸೆಷನ್‌ನಲ್ಲಿನ ಪ್ರತಿಯೊಂದು ರ್ಯಾಲಿಯನ್ನೂ ಹುಡುಕಿ, ನಾಕ್-ಅಪ್‌ಗಳು, ನಡಿಗೆ ಮತ್ತು ವಿರಾಮಗಳನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಹಾಕುತ್ತದೆ — ಹೀಗಾಗಿ ಪೂರ್ಣ ಪಂದ್ಯವು ನೀವು ನೋಡಿ ಡೌನ್‌ಲೋಡ್ ಮಾಡಬಹುದಾದ ಶುದ್ಧ ಹೈಲೈಟ್ ರೀಲ್ ಆಗುತ್ತದೆ. ಯಾವುದೇ ಎಡಿಟಿಂಗ್ ಇಲ್ಲ, ಸ್ಕ್ರಬ್ಬಿಂಗ್ ಇಲ್ಲ.

record.upload.body: ಸಮವಾದ ಬೆಳಕು, ನಂತರ <b>ಇಡೀ ಸೆಷನ್</b> ಅನ್ನು ರೆಕಾರ್ಡ್ ಮಾಡಿ ಇರುವಂತೆಯೇ ಅಪ್‌ಲೋಡ್ ಮಾಡಿ — KhelSutra ರ್ಯಾಲಿಗಳನ್ನು ಹುಡುಕಿ ಕತ್ತರಿಸುವ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡುತ್ತದೆ.

hero.title : हर रैली, सूची में. <span class="hl">बेकार समय, ग़ायब.</span>
hero.lead  : KhelSutra आपके सेशन की हर रैली ढूँढता है और नॉक-अप, चहलक़दमी और रुकावटें हटा देता है — जिससे पूरा मैच एक साफ़ हाइलाइट रील बन जाता है जिसे आप देख और डाउनलोड कर सकते हैं। कोई एडिटिंग नहीं, कोई स्क्रबिंग नहीं।
hero.ctaHow: देखें यह कैसे काम करता है
record.intro: हाइलाइट की क्वालिटी रिकॉर्डिंग से ही शुरू होती है। कैमरा एक बार ऐसे सेट कर दें और रैलियाँ ढूँढने के लिए KhelSutra के पास सब कुछ होता है।
record.upload.body: एक-समान रोशनी, फिर <b>पूरा सेशन</b> रिकॉर्ड करें और जैसा है वैसा ही अपलोड करें — KhelSutra रैलियाँ ढूँढता है और कटिंग करता है।
ownModel.heroLead: आप अपनी कोर्ट फ़ुटेज पर Gemini या GPT को ट्रेन नहीं कर सकते। KhelSutra की दिशा इसके उलट है — एक छोटा, <b style="color:#EAF3EE">अपना</b> रैली मॉडल जहाँ असली ताक़त आपका सहमति-वाला डेटासेट है, एक ऐसे कमर्शियली साफ़ स्टैक पर जिसे आप खुद जाँच और नियंत्रित कर सकें।
reveal.shareText: KhelSutra देखें — यह पूरे बैडमिंटन मैच को एक साफ़ हाइलाइट रील में बदल देता है 🏸 khelsutra.guru

========== es ==========
hero.title : Cada jugada, indexada. <span class="hl">El tiempo muerto, fuera.</span>
hero.lead  : KhelSutra encuentra cada jugada de tu sesión y elimina los calentamientos, las caminatas y las pausas — para que un partido completo se convierta en un reel de jugadas destacadas limpio que puedes ver y descargar. Sin edición ni rebobinados.
hero.ctaHow: Ver cómo funciona
record.intro: La calidad de las jugadas destacadas empieza con la grabación. Coloca la cámara así una sola vez y KhelSutra tendrá todo lo que necesita para encontrar las jugadas.
record.upload.body: Iluminación uniforme, luego graba la <b>sesión completa</b> y súbela tal cual — KhelSutra encuentra las jugadas y hace el recorte.
ownModel.heroLead: No puedes entrenar Gemini ni GPT con las grabaciones de tu cancha. La dirección de KhelSutra es la inversa — un modelo de jugadas pequeño y <b style="color:#EAF3EE">propio</b> donde la ventaja competitiva es tu conjunto de datos con consentimiento, sobre una base tecnológica comercialmente limpia e inspeccionable que tú controlas.
reveal.shareText: Mira KhelSutra — convierte un partido completo de bádminton en un reel de jugadas destacadas limpio 🏸 khelsutra.guru

========== da ==========
hero.title : Hver duel, indekseret. <span class="hl">Den døde tid, væk.</span>
hero.lead  : KhelSutra finder hver duel i din session og fjerner opvarmningsslag, gåture og pauser — så en hel kamp bliver til en ren highlights-reel, du kan se og downloade. Ingen redigering, ingen spoling.
hero.ctaHow: Se, hvordan det fungerer
record.intro: Kvaliteten af dine highlights begynder med optagelsen. Stil kameraet op én gang på denne måde, så har KhelSutra alt, hvad den skal bruge for at finde duellerne.
record.upload.body: Jævn belysning, optag derefter <b>hele sessionen</b> og upload den, som den er — KhelSutra finder duellerne og klarer klipningen.
ownModel.heroLead: Du kan ikke træne Gemini eller GPT på dine baneoptagelser. KhelSutras retning er den omvendte — en lille, <b style="color:#EAF3EE">ejet</b> duel-model, hvor fordelen er dit datasæt med samtykke, på en kommercielt ren, gennemskuelig stak, du kontrollerer.
reveal.shareText: Tjek KhelSutra — den forvandler en hel badmintonkamp til en ren highlights-reel 🏸 khelsutra.guru

hero.title : Setiap reli, terindeks. <span class="hl">Waktu kosongnya, hilang.</span>

hero.ctaHow: Lihat cara kerjanya
record.intro: Kualitas highlight dimulai dari rekamannya. Atur kamera sekali seperti ini dan KhelSutra sudah punya semua yang diperlukannya untuk menemukan reli.

ownModel.heroLead: Kamu tidak bisa melatih Gemini atau GPT pada rekaman lapanganmu. /* TEMP applier — adds hero-demo + #how i18n keys to all 6 catalogs.
   en values are DERIVED FROM THE WIRED DOM (byte-exact vs the drift guard);
   the other 5 locales come from the translation tables below. Deleted after run. */
const { JSDOM } = require("jsdom");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const ROOT = __dirname;
const htmlPath = path.join(ROOT, "site", "public", "index.html");
const { document } = new JSDOM(fs.readFileSync(htmlPath, "utf8")).window;

// 1) derive en map for the NEW keys straight from the DOM defaults
const NEWKEY = /^site\.(hero\.(ctaReel|note|loop|demo)|how\.)/;
const en = {};
for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
  if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue;
  const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
  const key = htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
  if (!NEWKEY.test(key)) continue;
  en[key] = (htmlKey ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
}

// 2) translations (kn/hi/es/da/id). Same dotted keys as en. Brand/tokens/numbers literal.

// 3) apply
const KEYS = Object.keys(en);
function setPath(obj, dotted, val) {
  const parts = dotted.split(".");
  let o = obj;
  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) {
    if (o[parts[i]] == null || typeof o[parts[i]] !== "object") o[parts[i]] = {};
    o = o[parts[i]];
  }
  o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = val;
}

const report = [];
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const p = path.join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json");
  const j = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
  const table = loc === "en" ? en : T[loc];
  // guard: every new key must be present in this locale's table
  const missing = KEYS.filter((k) => table[k] == null);
  if (missing.length) throw new Error(`${loc} missing translations: ${missing.join(", ")}`);
  for (const k of KEYS) setPath(j, k, table[k]);
  fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(j, null, 2) + "\n");
  report.push(`${loc}: +${KEYS.length} keys`);
}
console.log("Applied:\n" + report.join("\n"));
console.log("\nen keys derived from DOM:");
for (const k of KEYS) console.log("  " + k + " = " + JSON.stringify(en[k]));

### 78. user (2026-06-22T04:05:52.950Z)

1	# Live-host parity — the site runs on TWO hosts (Cloudflare Worker + the DO droplet's
2	# Node/SQLite twin) that must serve byte-identical pages. The unit suite proves the code
3	# works on each host; it CANNOT see that the two LIVE URLs have drifted (e.g. a merge
4	# auto-deployed Cloudflare but the droplet wasn't re-synced from git). This runs the
5	# existing scripts/parity-check.sh against both live URLs on a schedule — NOT on PRs, so
6	# it never blocks development; a failure surfaces real production drift to the owner.
7	name: live-parity
8	
9	on:
10	  schedule:
11	    - cron: "17 6 * * *" # daily ~06:17 UTC
12	  workflow_dispatch: {} # and on demand
13	
14	# Avoid overlapping runs if a manual dispatch races the cron.
15	concurrency:
16	  group: live-parity
17	  cancel-in-progress: false
18	
19	jobs:
20	  parity:
21	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
22	    steps:
23	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
24	      - name: Compare the two live hosts byte-for-byte
25	        env:
26	          CF_URL: https://khelsutra.guru
27	          VM_URL: http://168.144.126.176:8787
28	        run: bash site/scripts/parity-check.sh

/* TEMP: wire data-i18n into the 6 home-page sections. Pure string insertion (no DOM
   round-trip) so the HTML stays byte-stable except the added attributes. Each insertion
   asserts its anchor is globally unique. Deleted after run. */
const fs = require("fs");
const P = "site/public/index.html";
let html = fs.readFileSync(P, "utf8");
let n = 0;

// insert `attr="key"` at the end of the opening tag whose `>` immediately precedes `anchor`
function plain(anchor, key, attr = "data-i18n") {
  const find = ">" + anchor;
  const count = html.split(find).length - 1;
  if (count !== 1) throw new Error(`anchor not unique (${count}) for ${key}: ${JSON.stringify(find)}`);
  html = html.replace(find, ` ${attr}="${key}">` + anchor);
  n++;
}
// literal find->replace, assert once
function lit(find, replace, key) {
  const count = html.split(find).length - 1;
  if (count !== 1) throw new Error(`literal not unique (${count}) for ${key}: ${JSON.stringify(find)}`);
  html = html.replace(find, replace);
  n++;
}

// ---- #diff ----
plain("The hard problem we crack", "site.diff.kicker");
plain("Most academy halls run several courts at once", "site.diff.title");
plain("Academies already record a lot of video", "site.diff.p1");
plain("A single recording of a busy, multi-court hall", "site.diff.p2", "data-i18n-html");
plain("Capture tip", "site.diff.tipPill");
plain("Best results: a steady, full-court shot", "site.diff.tipTitle");
plain("Camera behind the court, slightly elevated", "site.diff.tipBody");

// ---- #sharper ----
plain("What makes it different", "site.sharper.kicker");
plain("It gets sharper from real academy footage", "site.sharper.title");
plain("The footage real academies contribute", "site.sharper.intro");
plain("Improves with the library", "site.sharper.improve.title");
plain("When you choose to share footage", "site.sharper.improve.body");
plain("Your data stays yours", "site.sharper.data.title");
plain("We never use your video to improve the tool without", "site.sharper.data.body");
plain("Minors are protected", "site.sharper.minors.title");
plain("Footage involving minors is never used", "site.sharper.minors.body");
plain("Read this honestly:", "site.sharper.honestLead");
plain("Today the tool gives you rally detection", "site.sharper.honestBody");

// ---- #ask ----
plain("Coming next to the alpha", "site.ask.kicker");
plain("Soon: ask for the exact reel you want", "site.ask.title");
plain("Your match is already a clean index of rallies", "site.ask.intro");
// q/a chips: wrap the text after the decorative icon in a localizable span (icon stays literal)
let qi = 0;
html = html.replace(/(<div class="q"><i>✦<\/i>)([\s\S]*?)(<\/div>)/g, (m, a, t, c) => { qi++; n++; return `${a}<span data-i18n="site.ask.q${qi}">${t}</span>${c}`; });
if (qi !== 3) throw new Error(`expected 3 .q chips, wrapped ${qi}`);
let ai = 0;
html = html.replace(/(<div class="a"><i>▶<\/i> )([\s\S]*?)(<\/div>)/g, (m, a, t, c) => { ai++; n++; return `${a}<span data-i18n="site.ask.a${ai}">${t}</span>${c}`; });
if (ai !== 3) throw new Error(`expected 3 .a chips, wrapped ${ai}`);
lit('<p class="ask-note">', '<p class="ask-note" data-i18n-html="site.ask.note">', "site.ask.note");

// ---- #roadmap ----
plain("Where it", "site.roadmap.kicker");
plain("Toward an AI assistant coach", "site.roadmap.title");
plain("Fed by the growing video library", "site.roadmap.intro");
plain("Shot detection", "site.roadmap.shot.title");
plain("Smashes, drops, net shots", "site.roadmap.shot.body");
plain("Deeper analysis", "site.roadmap.deep.title");

plain("Per-match history", "site.roadmap.history.title");

plain("Assistant coach", "site.roadmap.coach.title");

plain("Table tennis next", "site.roadmap.more.body");

// ---- #not ----

plain("What KhelSutra is not", "site.not.title");
plain("An early, invite-only trial", "site.not.li1");
plain("Not a line-calling, scoring", "site.not.li2");
plain("Not player tracking or identification", "site.not.li3");
plain("Not a replacement for a coach", "site.not.li4");

// ---- #faq ----

test("#how: the 'what it does today' feature block (head + 4 cards) is wired for translation", () => {

    assert.match(html, new RegExp(`data-i18n="site\\.how\\.${k}"`), `#how section head '${k}' isn't wired`);
  }
  for (const card of ["indexed", "deadtime", "reuse", "stitch"]) {
    assert.match(html, new RegExp(`data-i18n="site\\.how\\.${card}\\.title"`), `#how card '${card}' title isn't wired`);
    assert.match(html, new RegExp(`data-i18n="site\\.how\\.${card}\\.body"`), `#how card '${card}' body isn't wired`);
  }
  // The watermark + MP4 literals stay verbatim in the en source.
  assert.match(enCatalog.site.how.stitch.body, /“Generated by Khelsutra\.guru”/, "en how.stitch.body lost the verbatim watermark");
  assert.match(enCatalog.site.how.stitch.body, /\bMP4\b/, "en how.stitch.body lost the MP4 token");

  assert.ok(keys.length >= 11, `expected the full #how block wired (found ${keys.length})`);
  for (const key of keys) assert.notEqual(dig(enCatalog, key), undefined, `#how key "${key}" missing from en catalog`);
});

### 170. assistant (2026-06-22T04:51:49.853Z)

/* TEMP applier — writes the 65 new home-section keys into all 6 catalogs.
   en values come from _tmp_en_newkeys.json (byte-exact from the wired DOM);
   kn/hi/es/da/id come from _tmp_translations.json ({loc:{key:val}}) produced by the workflow.
   Deleted after run. */
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const ROOT = __dirname;

const en = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "_tmp_en_newkeys.json"), "utf8"));
const tx = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "_tmp_translations.json"), "utf8"));
const KEYS = Object.keys(en);

function setPath(obj, dotted, val) {
  const p = dotted.split(".");
  let o = obj;
  for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) {
    if (o[p[i]] == null || typeof o[p[i]] !== "object") o[p[i]] = {};
    o = o[p[i]];
  }
  o[p[p.length - 1]] = val;
}

const report = [];
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const p = path.join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json");
  const j = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
  const table = loc === "en" ? en : tx[loc];
  if (!table) throw new Error(`no translation table for ${loc}`);
  const missing = KEYS.filter((k) => table[k] == null || table[k] === "");
  if (missing.length) throw new Error(`${loc} missing ${missing.length} keys: ${missing.slice(0, 8).join(", ")}${missing.length > 8 ? " …" : ""}`);
  const extra = Object.keys(table).filter((k) => !en[k]);
  if (extra.length) throw new Error(`${loc} has ${extra.length} unexpected keys: ${extra.slice(0, 8).join(", ")}`);
  for (const k of KEYS) setPath(j, k, table[k]);
  fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(j, null, 2) + "\n");
  report.push(`${loc}: +${KEYS.length}`);
}
console.log("Applied home-section keys -> " + report.join(" | "));

### 173. assistant (2026-06-22T04:52:28.728Z)

/* TEMP structural verifier for the 65 home-section keys across kn/hi/es/da/id. Deleted after run. */
const fs = require("fs"), path = require("path");
const ROOT = __dirname;
const L = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"];
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const c = Object.fromEntries(L.map((l) => [l, load(l)]));
const en = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "_tmp_en_newkeys.json"), "utf8"));
const KEYS = Object.keys(en);
const dig = (o, k) => k.split(".").reduce((a, x) => (a == null ? a : a[x]), o);
const tagset = (s) => (String(s).match(/<[^>]+>/g) || []).slice().sort().join("|");
const KN = /[ಀ-೿]/, HI = /[ऀ-ॿ]/;
let fail = 0;
const eq = []; // (loc,key) that equal the en seed
for (const k of KEYS) {
  const ev = dig(c.en, k);
  if (ev !== en[k]) { console.log("EN-DRIFT", k, "(catalog != extracted DOM value)"); fail++; }
  for (const l of L.slice(1)) {
    const v = dig(c[l], k);
    if (v == null) { console.log("MISSING", l, k); fail++; continue; }
    if (tagset(v) !== tagset(ev)) { console.log("TAG DRIFT", l, k, "| en:", tagset(ev), "|", l + ":", tagset(v)); fail++; }
    if (v === ev) eq.push(l + " " + k);
  }
  for (const [l, re] of [["kn", KN], ["hi", HI]]) {
    if (!re.test(dig(c[l], k))) { console.log("NOT-IN-SCRIPT", l, k, "=>", dig(c[l], k)); fail++; }
  }
}
// token preservation
const need = [
  ["site.diff.p2", "KhelSutra"], ["site.sharper.intro", "KhelSutra"],
  ["site.faq.q7.a", 'href="#join"'],
  ["site.ask.a1", "1:32"], ["site.ask.q1", "10 seconds"],
];
for (const l of L) for (const [k, t] of need) { if (!String(dig(c[l], k)).includes(t)) { console.log("TOKEN LOST", l, k, "missing:", t); fail++; } }
// es/da/id cognate budget vs en seed (existing guard allows <= 8 TOTAL across the whole site.*)
const flat = (o, p = "") => Object.entries(o).reduce((a, [k, v]) => { const key = p ? p + "." + k : k; if (v && typeof v === "object") Object.assign(a, flat(v, key)); else a[key] = v; return a; }, {});
const enSite = flat(c.en.site), knSite = flat(c.kn.site);
const target = Object.keys(enSite).filter((k) => knSite[k] !== enSite[k]);
for (const loc of ["es", "da", "id"]) {
  const locSite = flat(c[loc].site);
  const stillEn = target.filter((k) => locSite[k] === enSite[k]);
  console.log(`${loc}: stillEn(site-wide) = ${stillEn.length} (guard <= 8)` + (stillEn.length > 8 ? " !!! OVER" : ""), stillEn.length ? "-> " + stillEn.slice(0, 12).join(", ") : "");
  if (stillEn.length > 8) fail++;
}
if (eq.length) console.log("\nNEW keys equal to en seed (review):", eq.join(" | "));
console.log(fail === 0 ? `\nSTRUCTURAL CHECK: PASS (${KEYS.length} keys x 5 locales)` : `\nSTRUCTURAL CHECK: ${fail} FAILURES`);
process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

### 270. user (2026-06-22T05:17:42.512Z)

### 271. assistant (2026-06-22T05:19:07.929Z)

Good instinct — three times in two days means it's worth a guard. Short version: **yes, we can, and it's worth it — but only the lean version.** Let me separate the two things "ensure rendering doesn't distort" could mean, because one is cheap and one is the trap:

**Why the current CI can't catch it:** all the site tests run in **jsdom, which has no layout engine** — `getBoundingClientRect()` returns zeros, so it literally cannot see overflow, wrapping, or clipping. Every header bug we hit (nav wrapping onto 3 lines, overlapping the brand, clipping the CTA) is invisible to jsdom by construction. To catch layout you need a *real* browser engine in CI.

**Two ways to do that:**

| | Lean layout-assertion guard ✅ | Full visual-regression (screenshot diff) |
|---|---|---|
| How | Playwright headless loads the pages at a few widths × 6 locales and asserts **invariants** | Captures screenshots, pixel-diffs against committed baselines |
| Catches our bug? | **Yes, directly** | Yes |
| Flakiness | None (geometry is deterministic; assertions are inequalities) | **High** — fonts/antialiasing differ Windows↔Ubuntu CI → false diffs |
| Upkeep | None | Re-bless baselines on *every* intentional UI change |
| Cost | ~1 devDep + `playwright install chromium` step, ~15s | Same deps + baseline storage + constant maintenance |

The full screenshot approach is the "too much" one — it's notoriously noisy across OS/font environments and you end up rubber-stamping baseline updates, which defeats the point. **The lean guard is the right call** and you already have Playwright in your toolbox (the screencast tooling + the preview I just used).

**What the lean guard would assert** (each of these would've failed CI on all 3 incidents):
- The header `.nav` doesn't horizontally overflow (`scrollWidth ≤ clientWidth`).
- On desktop the nav stays one line (height ≈ 66px — i.e. nav-links never wrap).
- The "Request access" CTA's right edge stays inside the viewport (not clipped).
- No page-wide horizontal scrollbar (`documentElement.scrollWidth ≤ innerWidth`) — a general "nothing overflows" net.
- Run across widths `[1280, 1100, 1000, 864, 768, 375]` × the 6 locales (the worst case is always the widest-autonym locale, id) on `index.html` / `capture.html` / `own-model.html`.

It runs as a new job in the existing `test` workflow, ~15s, no baseline management.

One scoping decision before I build it:

### 279. assistant (2026-06-22T05:23:57.228Z)

### 280. assistant (2026-06-22T05:24:21.972Z)

// Browser-layout regression guard (NOT part of `npm test` — it needs a real browser engine, so it
// runs as a separate `npm run test:layout` job in CI). The rest of the suite runs in jsdom, which
// has NO layout engine (getBoundingClientRect() is all zeros) and therefore cannot see overflow,
// wrapping, or clipping. This drives a headless Chromium over the REAL pages at a spread of
// viewport widths x all 6 locales and asserts the header never distorts — the exact class of bug
// that bit the responsive nav three times (links wrapping onto 3 lines + overlapping the brand, and
// the "Request access" CTA getting clipped off the right edge in wide-autonym locales like id).
//
// Assertions are layout INVARIANTS (inequalities with generous tolerances), not pixel screenshots —
// so they're robust across OS/font environments and need no baselines, while still catching the
// gross distortions that actually happened (50-75px of overflow / a 3-line nav).
import { test, before, after } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join, extname } from "node:path";
import { chromium } from "playwright";

const PUBLIC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public");
const TYPES = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".js": "text/javascript", ".mjs": "text/javascript",
  ".json": "application/json", ".css": "text/css", ".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml", ".webp": "image/webp", ".webm": "video/webm", ".mp4": "video/mp4",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2", ".woff": "font/woff", ".ico": "image/x-icon",
};

let server, browser, baseURL;

before(async () => {
  // Minimal static server for site/public (clean-URL fallback: /capture -> capture.html).
  server = createServer(async (req, res) => {
    try {
      let p = decodeURIComponent(req.url.split("?")[0]);
      if (p === "/") p = "/index.html";
      let data, ext = extname(p);
      try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p)); }
      catch { try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p + ".html")); ext = ".html"; } catch { res.writeHead(404); return res.end("not found"); } }
      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": TYPES[ext] || "application/octet-stream", "cache-control": "no-store" });
      res.end(data);
    } catch (e) { res.writeHead(500); res.end(String(e)); }
  });
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", r));
  baseURL = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  browser = await chromium.launch();
});

after(async () => {
  await browser?.close();
  await new Promise((r) => server?.close(r));
});

const PAGES = ["/index.html", "/capture.html", "/own-model.html"];
const LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]; // id (Bahasa Indonesia) is the widest-autonym worst case
const WIDTHS = [1280, 1100, 1000, 900, 768, 375]; // desktop + the old 860-1080 "dead zone" + tablet + phone

// Tolerances tuned to ignore sub-pixel / font-rounding noise while catching the gross distortions that
// actually occurred (the incidents were 50-75px). The nav-links height check is the strongest signal:
// hidden = 0px, one line ~= 24px, the wrapped-and-overlapping bug was ~75px.
const TOL = { overflow: 16, ctaClip: 8, navLinksMaxHeight: 44, docOverflow: 16 };

test("header never overflows / wraps over the brand / clips the CTA (pages x 6 locales x widths)", async () => {
  const failures = [];
  for (const path of PAGES) {
    for (const loc of LOCALES) {
      const page = await browser.newPage();
      try {
        await page.goto(`${baseURL}${path}?lang=${loc}`, { waitUntil: "load" });
        // Wait for the shim to apply the locale (so nav text is translated -> real widths) and for
        // webfonts to settle (text width is the whole point). en may already be the default.
        await page.waitForFunction((l) => document.querySelector("[data-langsw]")?.value === l, loc, { timeout: 6000 }).catch(() => {});
        await page.evaluate(() => (document.fonts ? document.fonts.ready.then(() => true) : true));
        for (const width of WIDTHS) {
          await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
          await page.waitForTimeout(70); // let the reflow settle
          const m = await page.evaluate(() => {
            const q = (s) => document.querySelector(s);
            const nav = q(".nav"), links = q(".nav-links"), de = document.documentElement;
            const cta = q(".nav-right .btn-dark") || q(".nav-right .btn");
            return {
              navOverflow: nav ? nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth : 0,
              navLinksHeight: links ? Math.round(links.getBoundingClientRect().height) : 0,
              ctaRight: cta ? Math.round(cta.getBoundingClientRect().right) : 0,
              docOverflow: de.scrollWidth - de.clientWidth,
            };
          });
          const at = `${path} [${loc}] @${width}px`;
          if (m.navOverflow > TOL.overflow) failures.push(`${at}: nav overflows horizontally by ${m.navOverflow}px`);
          if (m.navLinksHeight > TOL.navLinksMaxHeight) failures.push(`${at}: nav-links is ${m.navLinksHeight}px tall — wrapped onto multiple lines / overlapping the brand`);
          if (m.ctaRight && m.ctaRight > width + TOL.ctaClip) failures.push(`${at}: CTA right edge ${m.ctaRight}px is clipped past the ${width}px viewport`);
          if (m.docOverflow > TOL.docOverflow) failures.push(`${at}: page has ${m.docOverflow}px of horizontal overflow`);
        }
      } finally {
        await page.close();
      }
    }
  }
  assert.equal(failures.length, 0, failures.length ? `header layout distortions:\n  - ${failures.join("\n  - ")}\n` : "");
});

1	// KhelSutra marketing-site i18n shim (tracker B3) — vanilla, dependency-free, zero build.
2	//
3	// Mirrors the SPA's react-i18next contract (web/src/i18n/index.ts) for the static site:
4	//   - the SAME shared catalogs (repo-root /locales, copied into /public/locales — D-CATALOG-HOME);
5	//   - the SAME negotiation order: persisted (localStorage `khelsutra.lang`) -> ?lang= -> navigator -> en;
6	//   - ICU-lite interpolation `{name}` + plurals `{n, plural, one {…} other {…}}` via Intl.PluralRules
7	//     (CLDR-correct for en/kn/hi — the 2-form site languages — and any locale);
8	//   - a missing key degrades to en, then leaves the HTML's English default — NEVER a raw key.
9	//
10	// The pure helpers are exported for the unit test (src/i18n.shim.test.js); boot() runs only in a browser.
11	
12	export const SUPPORTED = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]; // marketing site now matches the SPA's 6 languages
13	export const STORAGE_KEY = "khelsutra.lang"; // shared with the SPA, so a choice carries across surfaces
14	export const DEFAULT = "en";
15	
16	const base = (code) => (code ? String(code).toLowerCase().split("-")[0] : ""); // "kn-IN" -> "kn"
17	const supported = (code) => (SUPPORTED.includes(base(code)) ? base(code) : null);
18	
19	// Negotiation — EXACTLY the SPA order. Anything unsupported is skipped; nothing matches -> en.
20	export function negotiate({ stored, query, nav } = {}) {
21	  const navList = Array.isArray(nav) ? nav : nav ? [nav] : [];
22	  for (const candidate of [stored, query, ...navList]) {
23	    const ok = supported(candidate);
24	    if (ok) return ok;
25	  }
26	  return DEFAULT;
27	}
28	
29	const dig = (obj, key) => key.split(".").reduce((o, k) => (o && typeof o === "object" ? o[k] : undefined), obj);
30	
31	// Resolve one plural block "{var, plural, one {…} other {…}}" (balanced braces, # = the number).
32	// Returns the string unchanged when there's no parseable plural block (so interpolate() terminates).
33	function plural(msg, vars, locale) {
34	  const m = msg.match(/\{(\w+),\s*plural,/);
35	  if (!m) return msg;
36	  let depth = 0, end = -1;
37	  for (let i = m.index; i < msg.length; i++) {
38	    if (msg[i] === "{") depth++;
39	    else if (msg[i] === "}" && --depth === 0) { end = i; break; }
40	  }
41	  if (end < 0) return msg;
42	  const inner = msg.slice(m.index + m[0].length, end);
43	  const n = Number(vars[m[1]] ?? 0);
44	  const cat = new Intl.PluralRules(locale).select(n);
45	  const arms = {};
46	  for (const a of inner.matchAll(/(\w+)\s*\{/g)) {
47	    let d = 1, j = a.index + a[0].length, e = -1;
48	    for (; j < inner.length; j++) { if (inner[j] === "{") d++; else if (inner[j] === "}" && --d === 0) { e = j; break; } }
49	    if (e < 0) break;
50	    arms[a[1]] = inner.slice(a.index + a[0].length, e);
51	  }
52	  const chosen = (arms[cat] ?? arms.other ?? "").replace(/#/g, String(n));
53	  return msg.slice(0, m.index) + chosen + msg.slice(end + 1);
54	}
55	
56	// ICU-lite: resolve every plural block, then simple {name} interpolation.
57	export function interpolate(msg, vars = {}, locale = DEFAULT) {
58	  if (typeof msg !== "string") return msg;
59	  let out = msg;
60	  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { const next = plural(out, vars, locale); if (next === out) break; out = next; }
61	  return out.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, k) => (k in vars ? String(vars[k]) : `{${k}}`));
62	}
63	
64	// active locale -> en fallback. undefined when absent in both (caller keeps the DOM's English default).
65	export function translate(catalogs, locale, key, vars) {
66	  let msg = dig(catalogs[locale], key);
67	  if (msg == null) msg = dig(catalogs[DEFAULT], key);
68	  return msg == null ? undefined : interpolate(msg, vars, locale);
69	}
70	
71	// Walk i18n-tagged nodes under `root`. Three forms (missing key -> skip, leaving the HTML default):
72	//   data-i18n="key"                      -> textContent
73	//   data-i18n="key" data-i18n-attr="x"   -> setAttribute(x, …)  (placeholder / aria-label / alt …)
74	//   data-i18n-html="key"                 -> innerHTML  (for catalog-authored inline markup like <b>;
75	//                                           values are our own content, never user input)
76	export function applyDom(root, catalogs, locale) {
77	  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
78	    const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
79	    const val = translate(catalogs, locale, htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n"));
80	    if (val == null) continue;
81	    const attr = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr");
82	    if (htmlKey) el.innerHTML = val;
83	    else if (attr) el.setAttribute(attr, val);
84	    else el.textContent = val;
85	  }
86	  // Reflect the active locale in the compact <select> language switcher(s).
87	  for (const sel of root.querySelectorAll("select[data-langsw]")) sel.value = locale;
88	}
89	
90	// ---- browser glue (skipped under the plain-node unit test, which has no document) ----
91	const readCookie = (name) => {
92	  const m = (document.cookie || "").match(new RegExp("(?:^|; )" + name.replace(/\./g, "\\.") + "=([^;]*)"));
93	  return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
94	};
95	
96	function persist(locale) {
97	  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, locale); } catch { /* private mode */ }
98	  // Mirror to a cookie so server.js (VM host) can honor the persisted pref server-side (no FOUC).
99	  try { document.cookie = `${STORAGE_KEY}=${locale};path=/;max-age=31536000;samesite=lax`; } catch { /* ignore */ }
100	}
101	
102	async function loadCatalog(loc) {
103	  try { const r = await fetch(`/locales/${loc}/common.json`); return r.ok ? await r.json() : {}; } catch { return {}; }
104	}
105	
106	let activeCatalogs = { en: {} };
107	let activeLocale = DEFAULT;
108	
109	async function render(locale) {
110	  const catalogs = { en: await loadCatalog("en") };
111	  if (locale !== "en") catalogs[locale] = await loadCatalog(locale);
112	  activeCatalogs = catalogs;
113	  activeLocale = locale;
114	  document.documentElement.lang = locale;
115	  applyDom(document, catalogs, locale);
116	  // Bridge for dynamically-injected content the static data-i18n walk can't reach (e.g. the
117	  // post-signup reel reveal): window.KhelSutra.t(key, vars) -> active-locale string (en fallback;
118	  // undefined if absent everywhere, so callers can supply their own default).
119	  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
120	    window.KhelSutra = { t: (key, vars) => translate(activeCatalogs, activeLocale, key, vars), lang: activeLocale };
121	  }
122	}
123	
124	async function boot() {
125	  let stored = null;
126	  try { stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY); } catch { /* ignore */ }
127	  if (!stored) stored = readCookie(STORAGE_KEY); // server-set or cross-store persisted pref
128	  const query = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("lang");
129	  const nav = navigator.languages && navigator.languages.length ? [...navigator.languages] : [navigator.language];
130	  const locale = negotiate({ stored, query, nav });
131	  await render(locale);
132	  document.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
133	    const sel = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("select[data-langsw]");
134	    if (!sel) return;
135	    const loc = supported(sel.value) || DEFAULT;
136	    persist(loc);
137	    render(loc);
138	  });
139	}
140	
141	if (typeof document !== "undefined" && typeof window !== "undefined") {
142	  if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
143	  else boot();
144	}

### 348. assistant (2026-06-22T05:46:06.238Z)

Done — and the guard earned its keep on day one. Summary:

## [khelsutra#91](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/91) — merged to master (`9f77ddc`)

**The CI guard you asked for** — a new `layout` job: headless Chromium (Playwright, a site test-only devDep) loads `index`/`capture`/`own-model` at **widths [1280,1100,1000,900,768,375] × all 6 locales** and asserts header **invariants** — no nav overflow, links never wrap to multiple lines, CTA not clipped, no page-wide horizontal scroll. Inequality assertions, **not screenshots** → robust across OS/fonts, no baselines, ~20s. (The reason the bug kept slipping through: every existing site test runs in jsdom, which has *no layout engine* — `getBoundingClientRect()` is all zeros — so it literally can't see overflow.)

**It immediately caught a bug I'd shipped.** The guard failed on first run: **#90's header fix was incomplete** — I'd verified en + id, but **es (+138px)** and **da (+47px)** still overflowed because their translated nav labels are much longer. So the "fixed" header was *still broken* on master for two locales. Exactly the kind of thing the guard exists to catch.

**The fix (auto-collapse, your pick):** the shim now measures the header after applying a locale (and on resize / once webfonts load) and collapses the 6-link nav to a clean brand + switcher + CTA row when the links won't fit. Because it's **measured live, it self-adapts to any future translation length** — this class of bug can't silently come back.

**Verified:**
- The guard **fails on the pre-fix CSS** (11 failures) and **passes after** — proven it actually catches the regression, not just rubber-stamps.
- **Green in CI's Ubuntu env** (53s) — confirms the tolerances hold across OS/fonts (the whole point of inequalities over pixel-diffing).
- In-browser: **en keeps all 6 links; es collapses cleanly** (no bare/broken look).
- `npm test` 85/85 (jsdom unit, incl. a `fitNav` safe-path test; the browser file is `*.browser.mjs` so it's not auto-run); web suite unaffected.

Merged, branches/worktree cleaned, main fast-forwarded, launch.json restored, handoff updated.

**Net:** you now have a guard that catches header distortion *and* a header that genuinely fits all 6 locales. So to directly answer your question — no, it wasn't too much; the *lean* version was right (full screenshot-diffing would've been the "too much" trap). Going forward, when you touch site CSS/markup, extend `site/src/layout.browser.mjs` rather than relying on a manual preview check.

(The engine `I18N_PLAN` assumed the indexer repo; this is the adaptation.)


=== §0 TL;DR / status line ===
## 0. TL;DR
A coach or parent in Bangalore should land on KhelSutra, feel at home, set up their camera with confidence, and get highlights — **in Kannada or Hindi**, with **zero hand-holding**. This project isolates that goal into one tracked workstream-pair: **(A)** make the UI super friendly for non-tech-savvy users, and **(B)** localize to Hindi + Kannada. Both surfaces (`web/` React SPA + `site/` vanilla marketing) have **zero i18n today** `[verified]`, so the foundation — shared `locales/{en,kn,hi}` catalogs + a negotiation contract + CI parity — is the real deliverable; translations follow. **The single most important caveat:** translations are **human-reviewed** (Gemini drafts are *inference only, never training* — the standing guardrail), and Kannada/Hindi rendering is **blocked on the OFL font decision (D-FONT)**. The cheapest unblocked high-impact first slice is the **SPA i18n scaffold + language switcher (en-only)** — it locks the shared catalog/negotiation contract and adds the render-test safety net the SPA currently lacks.
